# VFP Language Reference Coverage

- #4745 under #3217/#4691 covers a common VFPSource path idiom: no-argument `HOME()` now returns the configured directory with a platform-native trailing separator, allowing expressions such as `HOME()+"userlbls.dbf"` to resolve as intended. Dynamic `CREATE TABLE (expression) (fields)` is accepted alongside the `FREE` form. Focused runtime-surface and control-flow tests pass, and the packaged VFPSource `addlabel/addlabel.pjx` build/runtime reaches its normal `READ EVENTS` loop. This is an MVP compatibility slice, not a claim of complete `HOME()` overload or VFP runtime parity; the pending Apple numeric-parser change remains separate.

- Runtime parenthesized dynamic `DO` note: common VFP code uses `DO (cTarget)` and nested macro forms such as `DO (&cTargetHolder)`. Copperfin now evaluates these target expressions through the heap-backed iterative frame machine, including same-file and admitted project-local PRG dispatch with `WITH` arguments, under #3217/#4722. Static package dependency discovery remains literal-only, strict admission still fails closed for unadmitted files, and dynamic xAsset/control-flow forms remain separate.

- Runtime ReportListener initialization/configuration note: VFPSource UtilityReportListener calls `DODEFAULT()` and reads `HadError` during initialization, then invokes `GetConfigTable()` and `VerifyConfigTable()` for an existing `_ReportOutputConfig.dbf`. Copperfin now supplies the inherited initialization default, resolves the existing configuration file case-insensitively, validates the supported five-field configuration schema, and creates that schema on demand through `CreateConfigTable()` under #3217/#4717/#4718/#4719/#4739. The PRG parser also recognizes VFPSource's bare `NULL` sentinels under #3217/#4721, allowing the packaged ReportOutput sample to complete in normal and debugger-host modes. Missing, malformed, and unsupported tables return deterministic failure state; error-object parity and complete report configuration semantics remain separate.

- Runtime ReportListener configuration-table creation note: `CreateConfigTable()` is limited to ReportListener-derived objects and the portable five-field DBF schema. It creates missing tables, preserves existing tables unless the caller requests overwrite, and refuses writes in strict verified-byte sessions because a new destination is not an admitted immutable input. Index creation, generated sample records, and complete VFP report-configuration behavior remain separate under #3217/#4739.

- Runtime ReportOutput acceptance note: the real VFPSource `ReportOutput/reportoutput.PJX` builds with zero warnings and launches successfully in both normal and debug-host modes after the `CreateConfigTable()` slice. Normal execution reports `runtime.completed: true`; debug execution reaches completion with the generated event stream and exit 0. Exact-head Windows confirmation passed under closed issue #4739.

- Runtime strict ReportListener admission note: when verified-byte mode is enabled, `GetConfigTable()` resolves only admitted logical DBF bytes and `VerifyConfigTable()` parses an admitted temporary snapshot, including an admitted `.fpt` sidecar when present. A valid admitted table wins over tampered disk content, an admitted table can run with its physical files absent, and an unadmitted physical table is ignored under #3217/#4720. Ambiguous admission fails closed; non-strict lookup remains case-insensitive filesystem lookup.

- Runtime strict database admission note: `OPEN DATABASE` and local DBF reads now require exact normalized verified-byte override keys on POSIX under #3217/#4726, preventing a differently cased entry from being borrowed for a distinct case-sensitive path. Windows keeps case-insensitive verified matching; non-strict VFP path resolution remains case-insensitive.

- Runtime bare `NULL` note: VFPSource uses both `NULL` and `.NULL.` as null sentinels in assignments and `STORE` commands. Copperfin now maps the exact case-insensitive bare identifier `NULL` to the same null value as `.NULL.`, preserving `ISNULL()` and `VARTYPE() = 'X'` while leaving unknown identifiers uninitialized. Focused control-flow coverage and the real ReportOutput package validate the contract under #3217/#4721.

- Runtime literal `DO` dependency note: VFPSource projects may invoke a beside-project PRG that is not listed in the PJX, such as `DO wzastart` from `Wizards/wzapp/wzapp.PRG`. Copperfin now discovers and stages contained literal targets with VFP case-insensitive resolution under #110/#4714. The real `wzapp.PJX` package completes normal and debug Linux launches; dynamic DO expressions and xAsset forms remain separate.

- Runtime routine declaration note: VFP9 accepts `PROC name` as an abbreviation for `PROCEDURE name`. Copperfin now registers that form for same-file `DO` dispatch under #3217/#4715. The real VFPSource `ReportOutput/reportoutput.PJX` package reaches `PROC ReportOutputCleanup` in both normal and debug modes; the sample's caught OLE `Collection.Count` behavior remains a separate compatibility boundary.

- Runtime indirect STORE target note: VFPSource ReportOutput uses a preprocessor-defined target in `STORE ... TO ([OUTPUTAPP_REFVAR])`. Copperfin now expands only defined bracketed indirect STORE targets, preserving ordinary array targets under #3217/#4716. The real ReportOutput package reaches `Collection.Count` in normal and debug modes; the next caught boundary is the separate Utility/XML listener `GetConfigTable()` method path.

- Runtime control-flow terminator note: VFPSource uses whitespace-delimited trailing annotations after block terminators, including `ENDIF set('TALK') = 'ON'` and `ENDFOR nLoop = 1 TO 2`. Copperfin now recognizes these as terminators and does not execute the annotation suffix, preserving token-boundary behavior for lookalike identifiers under #3217/#4707. Linux and hosted Windows focused suites pass, and the real VFPSource `MemberDataEditor` package completes normally and in debug mode without a fault at `memberdataeditor.prg:38`; #4707 is closed.

- Runtime preprocessor whitespace note: VFP headers commonly separate `#define` names and values with tabs. Copperfin now treats all whitespace as a `#define` token separator, preserving lowercase directives and expanding tab-delimited constants inside class methods and TRY/CATCH error formatting. Hosted Windows seq384 confirms the real VFPSource ReportBuilder package stages the complete header chain and completes normal and debug launches under #4698; the issue is closed.

- Runtime typed-local object note: VFPSource `foxrefstart.prg` declares `oFoxRef AS FoxRef OF foxref.prg` and invokes it through `m.oFoxRef`. Copperfin now strips the declaration type suffix for local binding and resolves `m.name.Method()` against the local object under #3217/#4691. The local `foxref.PJX` package completes `SetProject()`, `SearchCount()`, and `ShowResults()` without a runtime fault. Full FoxRef project/editor integration and hosted UI behavior remain separate.

- Runtime `_SCREEN.MousePointer` note: VFPSource `foxrefstart.prg` reads and writes this application property during startup. Copperfin now exposes a shared headless `_SCREEN` / `_VFP` state contract with numeric `0` default, direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` reflection, nonnegative integer normalization, and built-in protection under #3217/#4690. The local VFPSource `foxref.PJX` run advances through the property and typed-local object startup boundaries. OS cursor selection, rendering, hit testing, and hosted UI behavior remain separate.

- Runtime `SET RELATION`/`SET SKIP` note: the local relation path now supports multiple `expression INTO child` clauses, `ADDITIVE` composition, explicit parent targets with `IN`, bare `SET RELATION TO` clearing, refresh after successful parent `REPLACE`/`UPDATE` mutations, and VFP relation introspection through `RELATION()`, `TARGET()`, `SET("RELATION")`, and `SET("SKIP")` under #3217/#4687/#4688/#4741. Introspection preserves declaration order, supports work-area/alias lookup, emits a restorable relation clause, and remains isolated by data session. VFP9 one-to-many skip state remains data-session scoped: `SET SKIP TO` marks existing links, supports multiple child aliases, clears only skip behavior, and advances the parent when child-side `SKIP` leaves the current matching group under #3217/#4686. Focused relation and forward/reverse headless navigation tests pass. Persistent DBC relations, child-side mutation policy, DELETE/RECALL behavior, interactive `BROWSE` row repetition, and broader multi-level behavior remain separate.

- Strict `DO FORM` xAsset note: security-enabled form startup now reaches the verified SCX/SCT snapshot path when the logical physical files are absent, consuming admitted immutable bytes and preserving the logical `form.open` event; missing admission still fails closed. Non-strict and explicitly authorized debug-manifest behavior remains unchanged under #3866/#4682. Report/label entry-point presence gates remain separate.

- Strict external PRG command-loading note: security-enabled `DO` and `CALL` now honor non-empty admitted source overrides even when the external `.PRG` path is absent, while missing admission fails closed through the existing verified-source diagnostic. Non-strict and explicitly authorized debug-manifest launches remain pathname-based; command tokens, stack-frugal frame behavior, and machine-readable contracts are unchanged under #3866/#4681.

- Strict external native PRG loading note: when a security-enabled runtime session has admitted source bytes for an external `.PRG`, `CREATEOBJECT()` and `NEWOBJECT()` now use that immutable source even when the logical file is absent from disk; missing admission still fails closed through the existing verified-source diagnostic. Non-strict and explicitly authorized debug-manifest launches retain their existing pathname behavior. This protects the stack-frugal native class activation path without changing VFP command tokens or machine-readable contracts under #3866/#4680.

- Runtime Grid `View` note: the mounted VFP9 topic `html/42a511bd-c4c8-4d25-b5f1-3928c7dcf9d3.htm` documents writable numeric `Grid.View` state, using Browse/Change combinations for unsplit and split panes. Copperfin now exposes a deterministic Browse (`0`) default through direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes, reflection, derived initialization, built-in protection, and normalization to `0..1` for an unsplit Grid or `0..3` when a positive partition state is present under #3217/#4663. Split-pane layout, painting, navigation/editing behavior, and designer serialization remain separate; the focused native runtime-surface target passes locally.

- Runtime Column dynamic font-style note: the mounted VFP9 topic `html/847b1aa1-69ea-41a4-8b27-2639684e6d96.htm` documents writable raw logical expressions for `Column.DynamicFontItalic`, `Column.DynamicFontStrikeThru`, and `Column.DynamicFontUnderline`, with false defaults. Copperfin now exposes these native PRG properties through direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes, reflection, explicit and derived declarations, and the materialized `Grid.Column1` path under #3217/#4661. Focused Linux runtime-surface CTest and exact-head Windows validation passed in coordination seq164; artifacts are under `artifacts/windows-validation-9fa13d61/`. Expression evaluation, grid refresh, per-row style resolution, font lookup, layout, rendering, host UI integration, and designer serialization remain separate.

- Runtime Form `DrawStyle` note: the extracted VFP9 `DrawStyle Property` topic applies the read/write numeric property to Form and `_SCREEN`, documents values `0..6`, and defines `0` (Solid) as the default. Supplied `FFC-master.zip` `_ui.vc2` saves/restores the host property and sets it to `0` while drawing control highlights. Copperfin now exposes native PRG Form `DrawStyle` with default `0`, direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` access, `0..6` normalization, derived-class preservation, and built-in reflection protection under #3217/#4571. Graphics methods, line-pattern rasterization, `_SCREEN` host state, and platform rendering remain separate work.

- Runtime visual `BackStyle` note: the extracted VFP9 `BackStyle Property` topic documents the read/write numeric property for CheckBox, CommandGroup, Container, EditBox, Image, Label, OptionButton, OptionGroup, Page, Shape, and TextBox, with `0` (Transparent), `1` (Opaque), and default `1`. Supplied `FFC-master.zip` sources use it extensively for transparent labels, shapes, and controls. Copperfin now exposes native PRG BackStyle state for those classes with direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` access, binary normalization, derived-class preservation, and built-in reflection protection under #3217/#4570. Transparency compositing, theme interaction, painting, and designer serialization remain separate work.

- Runtime visual `DrawMode` note: the extracted VFP9 `DrawMode Property` topic applies the read/write numeric property to Form, Line, and Shape, documents modes `1..16`, and defines mode `13` (Copy Pen) as the default. FFC `_ui.vc2` and `_reportlistener.vc2` use it while drawing control highlights and report visuals. Copperfin now exposes native PRG Form/Line/Shape `DrawMode` with default `13`, direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` access, `1..16` normalization, derived-class preservation, and built-in reflection protection under #3217/#4569. Bitwise compositing, graphics methods, and platform rendering remain separate work.

- Runtime/Form `ScaleMode` note: FFC `_ui.vc2`, `_movers.vc2`, `_html.vc2`, `_dialogs.vc2`, and `_therm.vc2`, plus VFPSource `Wizards/wzform/wizform.prg`, read or set `Form.ScaleMode` and commonly force mode `3` while performing pixel-coordinate layout before restoring the prior value. Copperfin now exposes native PRG Form `ScaleMode` with default `0`, direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` access, `0..3` normalization, derived-class preservation, and built-in reflection protection under #3217/#4568. Coordinate conversion, geometry recomputation, drawing, and platform rendering remain separate work.

- Report-header binary-protection note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines header-only `ORDER` as a memo containing 32 binary protection flags. Copperfin now preserves the exact memo bytes and exposes them as a stable uppercase `hex:` value with field/memo provenance through the shared report/label model under #24/#4543; edits decode only that contract, raw bytes bypass text code-page conversion, and clear/undo preserve byte identity. Flag interpretation, report-builder behavior, rendering, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-header uniqueness note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines header-only `UNIQUE` as the marker for a temporary or wizard-generated report. Copperfin now preserves the logical value and field/memo provenance and exposes a localized editable setting through the shared report/label model under #24/#4542. Report-generation/wizard behavior, rendering, hosted Windows VFP9 validation, and binary `ORDER` protection-flags interpretation remain separate. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-header position-state note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema uses header-only `CURPOS` for the saved Report Designer “Show Position” state; non-header records reserve it for Report Builder/runtime selection state. Copperfin now preserves the header value and field/memo provenance and exposes a localized editable setting through the shared report/label model under #24/#4541. Non-header builder/runtime semantics, cursor overlays, rendering, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-header alias-option note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines header-only `ADDALIAS` as the logical setting for adding field aliases. Copperfin now preserves its invariant value and field/memo provenance and exposes it as a localized editable setting through the shared report/label model under #24/#4540; Expression Builder alias behavior, `PLAIN`, `ORDER`, `CURPOS`, rendering, printer behavior, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-header printer-override note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema uses header `PICTURE` name/value pairs as printer overrides that take precedence over duplicate `EXPR` settings. Copperfin now preserves the effective pairs with source field/memo/line provenance and routes edits and clears to the selected backing memo under #24/#4539; object/image/expression `PICTURE` meanings and printer execution remain separate. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-header ruler-line note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines header-only `RULERLINES` for showing ruler lines. Copperfin now preserves the numeric value and field/memo provenance and exposes it as a localized editable setting through the shared report/label model under #24/#4538; expression-object `RULERLINES` remains the separate string-trimming field. Ruler-line rendering and measurement behavior remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-header view-settings note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines header-only `GRID` as the snap-to-grid flag and `RULER` as the ruler measurement-unit value. Copperfin now preserves these existing fields through the shared report/label settings model with invariant host settings, source-field provenance, localized editable property-grid fields, and generic edit/clear/reopen transport under #24/#4537. Grid/ruler rendering, snapping, measurement conversion, layout recalculation, printer behavior, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label font-charset note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `DOUBLE` as the explicit font-charset flag for picture, header, text/label, and expression records, and `RESOID` as the charset value for header, text/label, and expression records when applicable. Copperfin now preserves these existing fields through the shared report/label model with invariant host highlights, field/memo provenance, localized editable property-grid fields, and generic round-trip updates under #24/#4536. Font lookup, charset interpretation, glyph fallback, rendering, font-resource records, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report line/shape styling note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `PENSIZE` and `PENPAT` for Line and Shape objects (`OBJTYPE` 6 and 7), and `FILLPAT` for Shape objects (`OBJTYPE` 7). Copperfin now preserves live/deleted values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable numeric styling fields through the shared report/label model under #24/#4535; rendering, enum validation, painting, print behavior, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-control color-channel note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema stores foreground RGB channels in `PENRED`, `PENGREEN`, and `PENBLUE` for text/label and expression objects, and expression background RGB channels in `FILLRED`, `FILLGREEN`, and `FILLBLUE`. Copperfin now preserves live/deleted channel values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable numeric channels through the shared report/label model under #24/#4534; color interpretation, picker UX, rendering, dynamic color expressions, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label band-event note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines memo-backed `TAG` as a band On Entry run expression and `TAG2` as a band On Exit run expression. Copperfin now preserves live/deleted section values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable band event expressions through the shared report/label model under #24/#4533; control-object `TAG2` tooltip storage, event execution/compilation, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label optional-band note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `EJECTBEFOR` and `EJECTAFTER` for Title/Summary optional-band page-header/page-footer choices, and `PLAIN` for constant band height. Copperfin now preserves live/deleted section values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable optional-band flags through the shared report/label model under #24/#4532; optional-band execution, page emission, constant-height layout, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label section-pagination note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `PAGEBREAK`, `COLBREAK`, and `RESETPAGE` as logical band fields for starting on a new page, starting on a new column, and resetting page numbering. Copperfin now preserves live/deleted section values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable pagination flags through the shared report/label model under #24/#4531; pagination execution, page/column break rendering, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-control `TAG2` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema reserves memo-backed `TAG2` for tooltip text on text, line, shape, expression, picture, and group records (`OBJTYPE` 5, 6, 7, 8, 10, and 17). Copperfin now preserves live/deleted values and field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable `ToolTip Text` through the shared report/label model under #24/#4530; variable records remain excluded, while tooltip rendering and report runtime behavior remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report image `OFFSET` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema uses `OFFSET` on picture/bound records (`OBJTYPE=17`) to select the image source: `0` filename from `PICTURE`, `1` General field from `NAME`, and `2` expression from `NAME`. Copperfin now preserves live/deleted raw values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable `Image Source Mode` through the shared report/label model under #24/#4529; path resolution, General-field loading, expression evaluation, image loading/rendering, and hosted Windows validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report image `GENERAL` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema uses `GENERAL` on picture/bound records (`OBJTYPE=17`) for `0` clip, `1` scale while retaining shape, and `2` scale and fill frame. Copperfin now preserves live/deleted raw values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable `Image Scale Mode` through the shared report/label model under #24/#4528; image loading/scaling/rendering and path resolution remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label `SPACING` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema uses `SPACING` on text/label objects (`OBJTYPE=5`) for `0` single, `1` 1.5, and `2` double line spacing; expression records (`OBJTYPE=8`) store zero. Copperfin now preserves live/deleted label values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable `Line Spacing` through the shared report/label model under #24/#4527; expression records remain excluded and text measurement, line layout, and rendering remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-expression `RESETTOTAL` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema uses `RESETTOTAL` on calculated fields for reset timing: `1` end of report/innermost group, `2` end of page, `3` end of column, group values `6+`, and VFP9 detail-band values `80+`. Copperfin now preserves live/deleted raw values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable `Reset Total` through the shared report/label model under #24/#4526; reset execution, aggregation, pagination, and rendering remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-expression `TOTALTYPE` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines calculated field/report-expression totals as `0` none, `1` count, `2` sum, `3` average, `4` lowest, `5` highest, `6` standard deviation, and `7` standard variance. Copperfin now preserves live/deleted raw values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable `Calculation Type` through the shared report/label model under #24/#4525; `RESETTOTAL`, aggregation execution, pagination, and rendering remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-expression `FILLCHAR` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema reserves `FILLCHAR` on `OBJTYPE=8` fields for the expression data type (`N` numeric, `C` character, or `D` date). Copperfin now preserves live/deleted values and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable `Expression Data Type` through the shared report/label model under #24/#4524; blank values remain blank, while expression evaluation, formatting, and rendering remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-expression `OFFSET` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema uses `OFFSET` on `OBJTYPE=8` expressions for justification: `0` left, `1` right, and `2` center. The same field has separate shape-curvature and image-source meanings for other object types. Copperfin now preserves the invariant report-expression value and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable `Expression Alignment` in the shared report/label model under #24/#4523; native alignment rendering and text measurement remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report-expression `RULERLINES` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema reserves `RULERLINES` on `OBJTYPE=8` report expressions for string trimming. `0` or blank uses the default ellipsis-word behavior, `1` selects character trimming, `2` word trimming, `3` ellipsis-character, `4` ellipsis-word, and `5` ellipsis-path for filenames. Copperfin now preserves the invariant value and source field/memo provenance and exposes localized editable `String Trimming` in the shared report/label model under #24/#4522; native text measurement, trimming, and rendering remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label image `PICTURE` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema uses `PICTURE` on image objects (`OBJTYPE=17`) for a relative picture filename. Copperfin now preserves and exposes that invariant source value through the shared report/label model with field/memo provenance under #24/#4521, while keeping report-expression formatting, label alignment, and header-printer meanings separate. Path resolution, temporary-file handling, image loading/rendering/scaling, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label `PICTURE` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema uses `PICTURE` for report-expression (`OBJTYPE=8`) format/input-mask data and for label (`OBJTYPE=5`) alignment, with separate image and header-printer meanings. Copperfin now preserves and exposes the report-expression value through the shared designer model under #24/#4520, including invariant field/memo provenance; formatting evaluation, image rendering, and printer behavior remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label `MODE` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines object `MODE` as a numeric field combining opaque/transparent back-style values with left-to-right, right-to-left, or contextual direction flags. Copperfin now exposes the raw field as localized editable `Back Style / Direction Mode` and preserves native value/field provenance under #24/#4519; rendering, RTL layout, back-style painting, interpretation, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label `FONTSTYLE` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `FONTSTYLE` as a numeric bitmask whose documented style values may be combined. Copperfin now preserves this object field in native report-layout highlights with invariant value and source-field provenance under #24/#4518, while the existing shared editable property remains unchanged. Font rendering, bit interpretation, fallback, and hosted Windows VFP9 validation remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label `TOP` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines object `TOP` as the logical band-relative positioning flag used with `BOTTOM` and `FLOAT`; true fixes the object relative to the top of its band. The report header reuses the storage column for print area, so Copperfin exposes this field only on object selections in the shared report/label property grid under #24/#4517. Native highlights preserve value and field provenance; layout recalculation, floating behavior, rendering, and band geometry remain separate work. Reference: [VFP9 FRX/LBX schema](https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf).
- Report/label `BOTTOM` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `BOTTOM` as the logical object-position flag used with `FLOAT` and `TOP`; true fixes the object relative to the bottom of its band. Copperfin now exposes this existing field in the shared report/label property grid with localized display text, invariant `BOTTOM` updates, and preserved field provenance under #24/#4516. Native layout recalculation, floating behavior, rendering, and band geometry changes remain separate work. Reference: https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf.
- Report/label `SUPOVFLOW` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `SUPOVFLOW` as the logical Print When setting for detail overflow to a new page/column. Copperfin now exposes this existing field in the shared report/label property grid with localized display text, invariant `SUPOVFLOW` updates, and preserved field provenance under #24/#4515. Overflow evaluation, pagination, rendering, page/column breaks, and runtime report events remain separate work. Reference: https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf.
- Report/label `SUPRPCOL` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `SUPRPCOL` as the numeric Print When setting for the first whole band of a new page/column, with `3` enabled and `0` disabled. Copperfin now exposes this existing field in the shared report/label property grid with localized display text, invariant `SUPRPCOL` updates, and preserved field provenance under #24/#4514. Print When evaluation, page/column breaks, pagination, rendering, and runtime report events remain separate work. Reference: https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf.
- Report/label `SUPVALCHNG` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `SUPVALCHNG` as the logical Print When flag for printing an object only when its value changes, used with `SUPALWAYS`. Copperfin now exposes this existing field in the shared report/label property grid with localized display text, invariant `SUPVALCHNG` updates, and preserved field provenance under #24/#4513. Suppression evaluation, rendering, pagination, and runtime report events remain separate work. Reference: https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf.
- Report/label `SUPALWAYS` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `SUPALWAYS` as the logical Print When repeated-values setting. Copperfin now exposes this existing field in the shared report/label property grid with localized display text, invariant `SUPALWAYS` updates, and preserved field provenance under #24/#4512. Repeat-value suppression evaluation, `SUPVALCHNG`, pagination, rendering, and runtime report events remain separate work. Reference: https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf.
- Report/label `SUPGROUP` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `SUPGROUP` as the numeric group index used by the Print When “When this group changes” setting. Copperfin now exposes this existing field in the shared report/label property grid with localized display text, invariant `SUPGROUP` updates, and preserved field provenance under #24/#4511. Suppression evaluation, pagination, rendering, and runtime report events remain separate work. Reference: https://files.engineering.com/files/a54184ab-b7c8-4641-bfd9-c653a16588c7/90frx.pdf.
- Report/label `SUPEXPR` note: the VFP9 FRX/LBX schema defines `SUPEXPR` as the memo-backed object-level Print When expression. Copperfin now exposes this existing field in the shared report/label property grid with localized display text, invariant `SUPEXPR` updates, and preserved field/memo provenance under #24/#4510. Evaluation, rendering, suppression, pagination, and runtime report events remain separate work. Reference: https://files.engineering.com/files/47fff201-ac82-4da0-9340-ce0517ea76fa/90frx.pdf.
- Runtime/Column `Resizable` note: VFP9 documents a read/write logical `Column.Resizable` property with default `.F.`. Copperfin now exposes the built-in property for native PRG Column objects, including direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes, coherent reflection, declarative and derived `Init` support, logical normalization, and built-in property protection under #3217/#4509. Native column hit testing, dragging, painting, layout recalculation, and Resize event dispatch remain separate work. Reference: https://www.yaldex.com/fox_pro_tutorial/html/e1c98cb2-056b-4299-970a-9b88cb6d15a4.htm.
- Report/label stretching note: VFP9 report controls define `Stretch with overflow` and `Stretch relative to top` options. Copperfin now exposes these existing FRX/LBX logical values in the shared report/label property grid with localized display labels and invariant `STRETCH`/`STRETCHTOP` update targets; native stretching, band layout, pagination, and print semantics remain separate implementation work. Reference: https://www.vfphelp.com/help/_5wn12p5a4.htm.
- Report/label control option note: VFP9 report controls define `Float` and `NoRepeat` behavior fields. Copperfin now exposes these existing FRX/LBX logical values in the shared report/label property grid with localized display labels and invariant `FLOAT`/`NOREPEAT` update targets; rendering, layout, pagination, and repetition semantics remain separate implementation work.

This document anchors Copperfin's command/runtime backlog to the official Visual FoxPro reference instead of memory.

This is a reference-coverage document, not the active slice-selection authority. Choose active work from live GitHub issue state, `agents.md`, `agent-handoff.md`, and `docs/23-phase-a-dependency-breakdown.md`; use this file when the chosen slice touches VFP/runtime language coverage.

- Runtime reflection note: documented VFP `PUTPEM(oObject, cPEMName, eValue)` is the canonical write-by-name function. Copperfin accepts `PUTPEM()` on the shared runtime path and retains the historical `SETPEM()` spelling as a compatibility alias; new VFP-facing examples and coverage should use `PUTPEM()`.
- Runtime/Grid `AllowRowSizing` note: VFP9 documents a read/write logical Grid property that defaults to `.T.`, allowing run-time row resizing. Copperfin now exposes the built-in logical property for native PRG Grid objects with direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` coverage, coherent reflection, declarative and derived `Init` support, and built-in property protection under #3217/#4506. This does not claim interactive row dragging, native UI hit testing, painting, layout recalculation, or designer serialization.
- Runtime/Grid `AllowHeaderSizing` note: VFP9 documents a read/write logical Grid property that defaults to `.T.`, allowing run-time header resizing. Copperfin now exposes the built-in logical property for native PRG Grid objects with direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` coverage, coherent reflection, declarative and derived `Init` support, and built-in property protection under #3217/#4505. This does not claim interactive header dragging, native UI hit testing, painting, layout recalculation, or designer serialization.
- Runtime/Grid `HeaderHeight` note: VFP9 documents `Grid.HeaderHeight` as a writable pixel-height property, and real VFP usage commonly sets it to values such as `20` or `0` to hide the header. Copperfin now exposes the built-in numeric property for native PRG Grid objects with deterministic portable headless default `20`, finite integer normalization, direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` coverage, coherent reflection, declarative and derived `Init` support, and built-in property protection under #3217/#4504. This does not claim native header painting, font measurement, layout recalculation, interactive resizing, or designer serialization.
- Runtime/Grid `RowHeight` note: VFPSource uses `Grid.RowHeight` in real tooling, and the VFP9 property is a writable pixel-height state whose `-1` value requests automatic sizing. Copperfin now exposes the built-in numeric property for native PRG Grid objects with default `-1`, finite integer normalization, direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` coverage, coherent reflection, declarative and derived `Init` support, and built-in property protection under #3217/#4503. This does not claim native row painting, font-driven recalculation, interactive resizing, or designer serialization.
- Runtime/Grid `GridLineColor` and `GridLineWidth` note: VFP9 documents a read/write numeric Grid line color with default `0`, and a read/write line width with default `1` constrained to `1..40`. Copperfin now exposes both built-in numeric properties for native PRG Grid objects with direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` coverage, coherent reflection, declarative and derived `Init` support, range normalization, and built-in property protection under #3217/#4603. This does not claim grid-line rendering, hosted hit testing, layout recalculation, or designer serialization. References: [GridLineColor](https://www.yaldex.com/fox_pro_tutorial/html/edb89930-0554-40e8-a0dc-1b0a899b5792.htm), [GridLineWidth](https://www.vfphelp.com/vfp9/_5wn12pk8g.htm).
- Runtime/Grid `HighlightStyle` and `HighlightRowLineWidth` note: VFP9 documents `HighlightStyle` as a read/write numeric property with values `0..2` and default `0`, and `HighlightRowLineWidth` as a read/write numeric property with range `0..7` and default `1`. Copperfin now exposes both built-in numeric properties for native PRG Grid objects with direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` coverage, coherent reflection, declarative and derived `Init` support, range normalization, and built-in property protection under #3217/#4604. This does not claim selected-row focus behavior, color persistence, cell navigation, rendering, or designer serialization. References: [HighlightStyle](https://www.vfphelp.com/help/_5WN12PKCE.htm), [HighlightRowLineWidth](https://www.vfphelp.com/help/_5WN12PKC2.htm).
- Runtime/EditBox `MaxLength` note: real VFPSource Browser code assigns `.MaxLength = 0` on EditBox description controls. Copperfin now exposes the built-in numeric property for native PRG EditBox objects with default `0`, direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes normalized to nonnegative finite integers, derived `Init` support, coherent reflection, and built-in property protection under #3217/#4501. This does not claim native input enforcement, truncation, or UI behavior.
- Runtime/EditBox `ScrollBars` note: real VFPSource Browser code sets EditBox `ScrollBars` to `0` for empty descriptions and `2` when vertical scrolling is useful. Copperfin now exposes the built-in numeric property for native PRG EditBox objects with default `0`, direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes normalized to `0..3`, derived `Init` support, coherent reflection, and built-in property protection under #3217/#4500. This does not claim native scrollbar painting, viewport measurement, keyboard/mouse behavior, or pixel-level layout.
- Runtime/visual-control `FontShadow` note: Copperfin now seeds a deterministic headless logical default of `.F.` for native PRG visual controls and preserves logical values through direct access, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, reflection, and derived `Init` under #3217/#4469. Platform font lookup, rendering, designer serialization, and other font properties remain separate work.
- Runtime/visual-control `FontOutline` note: Copperfin now seeds a deterministic headless logical default of `.F.` for native PRG visual controls and preserves logical values through direct access, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, reflection, and derived `Init` under #3217/#4468. Platform font lookup, rendering, designer serialization, and other font properties remain separate work.
- Runtime/visual-control `FontStrikethru` note: Copperfin now seeds a deterministic headless logical default of `.F.` for native PRG visual controls and preserves logical values through direct access, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, reflection, and derived `Init` under #3217/#4467. Platform font lookup, rendering, designer serialization, and other font properties remain separate work.
- Runtime/visual-control `FontUnderline` note: Copperfin now seeds a deterministic headless logical default of `.F.` for native PRG visual controls and preserves logical values through direct access, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, reflection, and derived `Init` under #3217/#4466. Platform font lookup, rendering, designer serialization, and other font properties remain separate work.
- Runtime/visual-control `FontItalic` note: Copperfin now seeds a deterministic headless logical default of `.F.` for native PRG visual controls and preserves logical values through direct access, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, reflection, and derived `Init` under #3217/#4465. Platform font lookup, rendering, designer serialization, and other font properties remain separate work.
- Runtime/visual-control `FontBold` note: Copperfin now seeds a deterministic headless logical default of `.F.` for native PRG visual controls and preserves logical values through direct access, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, reflection, and derived `Init` under #3217/#4464. Platform font lookup, rendering, designer serialization, and other font properties remain separate work.
- Runtime/visual-control `FontSize` note: Copperfin now seeds a deterministic headless numeric default of 10.0 for native PRG visual controls, preserves fractional nonnegative values through direct access, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, reflection, and derived `Init`, and normalizes invalid writes to 0.0 under #3217/#4463. Platform font lookup, rendering, designer serialization, and other font properties remain separate work.
- Runtime/visual-control `FontName` note: Copperfin now seeds a deterministic headless `Arial` default for native PRG visual controls and preserves string values through direct access, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, reflection, and derived `Init` under #3217/#4462. Platform font lookup, rendering, designer serialization, and other font properties remain separate work.
- Runtime/visual-control `FontCharSet` note: the mounted VFP9 `dv_foxhelp.chm` topic `html/8ac09fef-2dc1-44be-86a1-5592d41c4a68.htm` documents this read/write numeric property for Form and multiple font-bearing controls, and specifies value `1` as the default-setting value. Copperfin now exposes native PRG visual `FontCharSet` state under #3217/#4638 with direct and reflective mutation, derived-class preservation, finite nonnegative integer normalization, and built-in reflection protection. OS font lookup, charset substitution, glyph rendering, font-dialog behavior, and full Page/`_SCREEN` applicability remain separate evidence-backed work.
- Runtime/visual-control `MousePointer` note: VFP visual controls expose a numeric, writable `MousePointer` property with default 0. Copperfin now seeds and reflects that property for native PRG visual controls, normalizes direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes to nonnegative integer state, and preserves derived `Init` values under #3217/#4461. OS cursor selection, MouseIcon assets, and pixel-level UI behavior remain separate work.
- Runtime/list-control focus note: native ListBox multi-select now preserves `ListIndex` as the focused row when `Selected(n) = .F.` clears that row, even when another row remains selected. Focused-row `ListItemID`, `Value`, and `DisplayValue` remain coherent with independent selection bits, while single-selection controls retain their existing fallback behavior; focused and derived-Init regressions are covered under #3217/#4420.
- Runtime/list-control `Value` assignment note: mounted VFP9 samples assign `ListBox.Value` directly to select an existing value. Copperfin now routes populated ComboBox/ListBox `Value` and `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes through live selection state, matching the effective `BoundColumn` for multicolumn rows and preserving the existing numeric index-value mode. Unmatched values leave the prior selection unchanged; empty controls retain raw Value assignment behavior under #3217/#4436. This slice does not claim native incremental-search, popup, or UI selection behavior.
- Runtime/list-control `ItemData` note: the mounted/extracted VFP9 help topic documents numeric, read/write `ItemData(nIndex)` values for ComboBox/ListBox rows. Copperfin now preserves that per-row state through ordinary and bracket selectors, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()`, reflection, add/sort/remove/clear operations, and derived-class `Init` flows under #3217/#4426. This slice does not claim `TopItemID`/scrolling behavior.
- Runtime/list-control `TopItemID` note: the mounted/extracted VFP9 help topic documents the item ID shown in the top-most list position, with ComboBox read-only behavior and ListBox runtime writes. Copperfin now keeps the modeled item-ID state coherent through add, sorted reorder, removal, clear, reflection, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()`, custom IDs, and derived `Init` under #3217/#4427. This slice does not claim pixel-level scrolling, viewport painting, keyboard navigation, or `TopIndex` parity.
- Runtime/list-control `TopIndex` note: the mounted/extracted VFP9 help topic documents the 1-based index shown in the top-most list position, with the same ComboBox read-only/ListBox runtime-write rule as `TopItemID`. Copperfin now keeps `TopIndex` and `TopItemID` mutually coherent through add, sorted reorder, removal, clear, reflection, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()`, custom IDs, and derived `Init` under #3217/#4428. This slice does not claim pixel-level scrolling, viewport painting, keyboard navigation, or scroll-event parity.
- Runtime/list-control `RowSourceType` correction: the field-structure lane is implemented under #3217/#4430. `Requery()` supports values 1 through 8, including selected-cursor or explicit open-alias field-name materialization for type 8.
- Runtime/list-control collection correction: `RowSourceType = 10` is implemented under #3217/#4431. `Requery()` evaluates the collection expression, preserves keyed and unkeyed member order, displays scalar members in the first column, uses `(Object)` for object members without requested properties, and exposes requested object-property columns.
- Runtime/list-control popup correction: `RowSourceType = 9` now has a first-pass static lane under #3217/#4595 plus bounded macro-backed popup-name resolution under #3217/#4605. `DEFINE POPUP` plus numeric `DEFINE BAR ... PROMPT ...` declarations materialize prompt rows in bar-number order, with same-name replacement, `RELEASE POPUP` cleanup, re-evaluation of simple or nested memory-variable indirection at `Requery()` time, and empty missing-popup behavior. Static `ON SELECTION BAR n OF popup DO routine` declarations now register session-local callbacks, while static `ON SELECTION POPUP popup DO routine` provides the documented fallback when a valid bar has no bar-specific callback; `ON SELECTION BAR` retains precedence and the no-command popup form clears the fallback under #3217/#4628/#4629. Static literal `ON SELECTION BAR n OF popup <command>` assignments and expression actions now dispatch through the iterative frame path when no `DO routine` callback takes precedence under #3217/#4633. First-pass `DEACTIVATE POPUP` and `DEACTIVATE MENU` now end the waiting surface without clearing definitions, callbacks, skip/mark state, or prompt rows under #3217/#4630; `RELEASE` remains destructive cleanup. Static `ON BAR n OF popup ACTIVATE POPUP submenu` declarations now activate a defined child popup when no selection callback takes precedence under #3217/#4631. The headless runtime rejects missing, separator, skipped, or unregistered bars and emits stable selection/lifecycle telemetry. `ALL`, dynamic/macro command expressions, `ACTIVATE MENU`, arbitrary commands beyond the deactivation, static submenu, and static action forms, deeper nested activation, keyboard/UI interaction, and UI rendering remain separate.
- Runtime/menu `PRMBAR()` note: VFP9 documents `PRMBAR(cMenuName, nMenuItemNumber)` as a character lookup over defined popup bars. Copperfin now resolves the existing session-scoped `DEFINE POPUP` / `DEFINE BAR ... PROMPT ...` table, preserves one-based bar numbering, strips access-key and disabled markers, and returns an empty string for separators, missing menus, or out-of-range bars under #3217/#4614. System-menu lookup, menu rendering, and broader menu APIs remain separate.
- Runtime/menu `CNTBAR()` note: VFP9 documents `CNTBAR(cMenuName)` as a numeric count for defined popup bars. Copperfin now counts the existing session-scoped bar table, including sparse bar numbers, and returns zero for missing menus under #3217/#4615. System-menu counts, activation-time prompt menus, rendering, and broader menu APIs remain separate.
- Runtime/menu `GETBAR()` note: VFP9 documents `GETBAR(cMenuName, nMenuPosition)` as the actual bar number occupying a one-based popup position. Copperfin now maps sorted session-scoped popup bars to their sparse bar numbers and returns zero for missing or out-of-range positions under #3217/#4616. System-menu lookup, menu reordering commands, rendering, and broader menu APIs remain separate.
- Runtime/menu `SET SKIP OF BAR` / `SKPBAR()` note: VFP9 documents `SET SKIP OF BAR n OF menu lExpression` as the disabled-state setter and `SKPBAR(cMenuName, nMenuItemNumber)` as its logical query. Copperfin now stores that state per session, popup, and actual bar number, clears it on popup redefinition/release, and returns false for enabled or missing bars under #3217/#4617. Menu rendering, dimming, interactive selection, system menus, and PAD/POPUP/MENU variants remain separate.
- Runtime/menu `SET MARK OF BAR` / `MRKBAR()` note: VFP9 documents `SET MARK OF BAR n OF menu TO lExpression` as the marked-state setter and `MRKBAR(cMenuName, nMenuItemNumber)` as its logical query. Copperfin now stores that state per session, popup, and actual bar number, clears it on popup redefinition/release, and returns false for unmarked or missing bars under #3217/#4618. Checkmark rendering, system menus, and MENU/POPUP variants remain separate.
- Runtime/list-control `MoveItem` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents `ListBox.MoveItem(nItemIndex, nMoveBy)` and limits movement to `RowSourceType` 0 or 1. Copperfin now moves the complete row bundle by a signed, clamped offset while preserving item IDs, `ItemData`, selection, `ListIndex`, `NewIndex`, and modeled `TopIndex`/`TopItemID` under #3217/#4432; popup and pixel-level UI behavior remain separate work.
- Runtime/list-control `OnMoveItem` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents `nSource, nShift, nCurrentIndex, nMoveBy`; programmatic `MoveItem()` calls use `8, 0,` followed by the method arguments, raise the event once even for unsupported or invalid movement, and allow a false result to veto movement. Copperfin now passes those parameters to same-PRG handlers and bound delegates through the iterative frame path under #3217/#4433/#4434; stable row state and machine contracts remain invariant.
- Runtime/list-control `MoverBars` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents a logical, read/write `ListBox.MoverBars` property that defaults to false and is available only for `RowSourceType` 0 and 1. Copperfin now seeds and reflects the built-in property for plain and derived ListBox objects, rejects writes for unsupported row-source modes, and keeps the value logical under #3217/#4435. This property-contract slice does not claim mover-bar painting or interactive mouse/keyboard behavior.
- Runtime/list-control `AutoHideScrollBar` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents a read/write integer `ListBox.AutoHideScrollBar` property, defaulting to 0 (always show) with 1 meaning show only when the list cannot fully display its items. Copperfin now seeds and reflects the ListBox-only built-in property, normalizes writes to 0/1 through direct assignment and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, and blocks `ADDPROPERTY()` / `REMOVEPROPERTY()` shadowing under #3217/#4437. This property-contract slice does not claim scrollbar painting, viewport measurement, or native visibility behavior.
- Runtime/list-control array-range note: mounted VFP9 help documents `FirstElement` and `NumberOfElements` for array-backed ComboBox/ListBox sources. Copperfin now exposes both built-in numeric properties, normalizes their direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes, and applies the requested start/count window during one-column `RowSourceType = 5` `Requery()` while leaving multicolumn rows intact under #3217/#4438. This state slice does not claim popup sizing, viewport painting, or keyboard/scroll behavior.
- Runtime/combobox `DisplayCount` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents a read/write ComboBox property defaulting to 0. Copperfin now exposes the logical built-in numeric property, normalizes direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes, and blocks reflection shadowing under #3217/#4439. This property-contract slice does not claim popup sizing, scroll-arrow painting, or native UI behavior.
- Runtime/list-control `NullDisplay` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents a read/write ComboBox/ListBox text property defaulting to `.NULL.`. Copperfin now preserves the underlying null `Value` while using the object-specific `NullDisplay` text for selected-row `DisplayValue`, with direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` reflection protection under #3217/#4440. Global `SET NULLDISPLAY`, native UI painting, and unrelated database null propagation remain separate work.
- Runtime/list-control `ColumnLines` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents a read/write logical ComboBox/ListBox property defaulting to `.T.`. Copperfin now seeds and reflects the built-in value, normalizes direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes, and blocks property shadowing under #3217/#4441. Column-separator painting remains separate UI work.
- Runtime/list-control `ItemTips` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents a read/write logical ComboBox/ListBox property defaulting to `.F.`. Copperfin now seeds and reflects the built-in value, normalizes direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes, and blocks property shadowing under #3217/#4448. Tooltip rendering and hover behavior remain separate UI work.
- Runtime/list-control `IncrementalSearch` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents a read/write logical ComboBox/ListBox property defaulting to `.T.`. Copperfin now seeds and reflects the built-in value, normalizes direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes, and blocks property shadowing under #3217/#4451. Keyboard/prefix-search behavior and native UI interaction remain separate work.
- Runtime/text-entry `SelectOnEntry` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents a read/write logical Column/EditBox/TextBox property, defaulting to `.T.` for Column and `.F.` for EditBox/TextBox. Copperfin now seeds and reflects those class-specific defaults, normalizes direct and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()` writes, and blocks property shadowing under #3217/#4452. Focus, highlighting, and text-selection behavior remain separate UI work.
- Runtime/ListBox `IntegralHeight` note: the mounted VFP9 help topic documents a logical ListBox property defaulting to `.F.` and read-only at run time. Copperfin now seeds and reflects the built-in property, reports read-only reflection, and rejects direct assignment, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, and property shadowing under #3217/#4449. Pixel-level height adjustment and the EditBox/TextBox surfaces remain separate work.
- Runtime/text-control `IntegralHeight` note: the same mounted VFP9 help topic also applies the logical, run-time read-only property to EditBox and TextBox, with default `.F.`. Copperfin now seeds and reflects those two built-in surfaces and rejects direct assignment, `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PUTPEM()`, and property shadowing under #3217/#4453. Pixel-level height adjustment and native layout remain separate work.

## Current Copperfin Runtime Surface

The native runtime/parser currently has first-pass support for these command families:

- Runtime/parser note: first-pass preprocessor handling now follows nearby `#INCLUDE` headers recursively with cycle protection, tolerates Windows-style backslash include paths, captures local and included `#DEFINE` constants, expands bare identifier constants outside string/bracket/brace literals, and now honors nested `#IF`, `#ELSE`, `#ENDIF`, `#IFDEF`, and `#IFNDEF` selection before ordinary PRG parsing. This intentionally covers common header-driven constant and guard/debug-selection paths such as `WINDOWTYPE_MODAL`, `DEFAULT_MBOX_TITLE_LOC`, and `COMPILEBEFORE` evidenced in mounted/extracted VFP9 and FFC sources, but it does not yet claim full FoxPro conditional-preprocessor parity or arbitrary compile-time function evaluation.
- Runtime/parser build-hardening note: the native PRG parser's common IF/ELSEIF/FOR/TRY/CATCH recognition now lives in a separate helper under #110/#4632. This preserves statement kinds and expressions while keeping the remaining dispatch below the MSVC C1061 nested-block limit; the change is a compiler-structure fix only and does not alter PRG tokens, machine contracts, or the iterative stack-frugal execution model.
- Runtime string-compatibility note: #4744 adds the VFP9 C-suffixed character-safe family `LENC()`, `LEFTC()`, `RIGHTC()`, `ATCC()`, `RATC()`, `SUBSTRC()`, and `STUFFC()`. Their positions and lengths count validated UTF-8 scalar sequences rather than raw bytes, zero/negative `SUBSTRC()` starts clamp to scalar position 1, zero/negative `STUFFC()` starts preserve the existing insertion behavior, ASCII case folding does not corrupt multibyte data, invalid UTF-8 bytes remain one safely sliceable unit, and the existing byte-oriented `ATC()` path remains unchanged. The slice is grounded by the mounted VFP9 `genmenu.prg` use of `SUBSTRC()` and the generated official help inventory; focused ASCII/multibyte/occurrence/replacement/start-clamping tests pass on Linux. Exact-head Windows and later macOS validation remain separate platform evidence.
- Runtime/data-navigation note: local `SET RELATION` forms register relations per data session and synchronize children after parent `GO`, `SKIP`, `SEEK`, and successful parent `REPLACE`/`UPDATE` commands under #109/#4673/#4687/#4688. The path covers replacement and `OFF INTO <child>`, multiple clauses, `ADDITIVE`, explicit parent targets, and mutation refresh; the child uses its active order or first available order, exact-key behavior remains bounded by the existing seek contract, and missing child orders deterministically leave the child at EOF. This slice does not claim persistent DBC relations, child-side mutation policy, DELETE/RECALL refresh policy, interactive BROWSE repetition, or broader multi-level relation graphs.
- Runtime/query note: direct `SELECT ... INTO ARRAY` command forms now route through the shared query materializer under #4691/#3217. The current slice covers `CNT(*)`, `WHERE` predicates, object-backed `(THIS.RefTable)` sources, `_TALLY`, selected-work-area preservation, source cursor snapshot restoration, and array assignment without changing machine-readable contracts. Focused control-flow coverage passes, and the packaged VFPSource FOXREF run completes after this path. Grouped/joined query parity and broader INSERT query coverage are separate work.
- Runtime/list-control note: first-pass native `ComboBox`/`ListBox` list-control row-state support now exists on the iterative PRG runtime path, directly evidenced by mounted VFP9 sample `samples/solution/forms/graphics/fdproc.prg`, extracted FFC usage in `_controls.vc2`, `_table.vc2`, `_internet.vc2`, `_ws3utils.vc2`, `_movers.vc2`, and extracted VFP source usage in `VFPSource/builders/wbgrid.prg`, plus the local VFP9 help topics for `AddItem Method (Visual FoxPro)`, `AddListItem Method`, `Clear Method`, `ItemData Property`, `ListCount Property`, `List Property`, `ListItemID Property`, `NewIndex Property`, `NewItemID Property`, `RemoveItem Method`, `RemoveListItem Method`, `Selected Property`, `SelectedID Property`, `TopIndex Property`, and `TopItemID Property`. The shipped baseline covers same-session single-column runtime population, explicit 1-based indexed insertion, representative multicolumn `AddItem(..., nIndex, nColumn)` row insertion into the requested representative column, `RowSourceType` values 1 through 10 through `Requery()` including value, alias, SQL, query-file, array, field, file-mask, field-structure, static popup, macro-backed popup-name, and collection materialization, builtin `Clear()` behavior for `RowSourceType = 0` with a no-op gate on other row-source modes, read-only `ListCount`, read-only `NewIndex`, read-only `NewItemId`, representative single-column and multicolumn `List(nIndex, nColumn)` reads, representative writable `List(NewIndex, nColumn)` addressing across row insert/remove churn, writable `ListItemID` selection routing, representative `ListBox.Selected(nIndex)` reads/writes over preserved per-row selection bits, representative `SelectedID(nItemID)` reads/writes over the same live item-id row state, numeric per-row `ItemData(nIndex)` reads/writes through ordinary and bracket selectors plus `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()`, modeled `TopItemID`/`TopIndex` paired reads/writes with documented ComboBox/ListBox read-only behavior, representative `RemoveListItem(nItemID)` removal by stable row id, built-in reflection for `BoundTo`, `ListBox.BoundColumn`, `ListBox.ColumnCount`, `ListBox.ColumnWidths`, `ItemData`, `TopItemID`, `TopIndex`, `AddItem()` / `AddListItem()` / `Clear()` / `RemoveItem()` / `RemoveListItem()` plus `ListItemID`, `SETPEM(..., 'Selected(n)', ...)`, and `SETPEM(..., 'SelectedID(n)', ...)` through `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PEMSTATUS()` / `AMEMBERS()`, and coherent `ListIndex` / `NewIndex` / `ListItemID` / `DisplayValue` synchronization when rows are inserted, shifted, selected, removed, re-queried, or cleared. It intentionally does not yet claim popup interaction or selection callbacks, separators, pixel-level top-row scrolling, viewport painting, keyboard navigation, scroll-event parity, or complete sorted-list semantics outside the covered per-row state; unresolved sources remain empty instead of fabricating partial state.
- Runtime/pageframe note: representative same-session native `PageFrame` collection parity now exists on the iterative PRG runtime path, evidenced by the local VFP9 help topics for `Pages Property`, `PageCount Property`, and `ActivePage Property` together with repo-local extracted-source citations that use `Pages[m.j]`. The shipped baseline now synthesizes a hidden read-only `Pages` collection over live `Page` children, preserves `Pages.Count`, default-item `Pages(n)` access, bracket `Pages[n]` access, and `FOR EACH ... FOXOBJECT` iteration without leaking the synthetic `Collection` object into exported runtime state, keeps read-only `PageCount` synchronized through same-session page add/remove flows, and now exposes first-pass writable `ActivePage` selection with 1-based bounded page indexes, deterministic empty-page `0` behavior, and reflection coverage through `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PEMSTATUS()` / `AMEMBERS()`.
- Runtime/tab-order note: first-pass native `TabIndex` property parity now exists on the iterative PRG runtime path for the documented VFP9 help applies-to set that Copperfin currently models as same-session native visual controls. Evidenced by the mounted VFP9 `TabIndex Property` topic from `dv_foxhelp.chm`, the shipped baseline seeds built-in `TabIndex` values by add-order, preserves class-defined overrides, keeps ordinary reads/writes and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PEMSTATUS()` / `AMEMBERS()` coherent for plain, child, nested-child, and derived controls, and blocks `ADDPROPERTY()` / `REMOVEPROPERTY()` from shadowing or erasing the built-in surface. This slice is intentionally property-surface-only and does not yet claim full keyboard-navigation or tab-stop traversal parity.
- Runtime/tab-order note: first-pass native `TabStop` property parity now exists on the iterative PRG runtime path for the documented VFP9 help applies-to set that Copperfin currently models as same-session native visual controls. Evidenced by the mounted VFP9 `TabStop Property` topic from `dv_foxhelp.chm`, the shipped baseline seeds built-in `TabStop = .T.` state by default, preserves class-defined overrides, keeps ordinary reads/writes and `GETPEM()` / `SETPEM()` / `PEMSTATUS()` / `AMEMBERS()` coherent for plain, child, nested-child, and derived controls, and blocks `ADDPROPERTY()` / `REMOVEPROPERTY()` from shadowing or erasing the built-in surface. This slice is intentionally property-surface-only and does not yet claim full keyboard-navigation or focus-traversal parity.

- E2 designer/tooling note — aggregate designer dispatch execution catalog planning now reports explicit aggregate execution-admission readiness and per-context preflight errors through `plan_studio_designer_dispatch_execution_catalog` without launching child executors, builders, editors, toolbox processes, or UI, and Studio host exposes that non-mutating readiness through `--designer-dispatch-execution-catalog`; builder dispatch execution catalog planning reports explicit execution-admission readiness and per-builder preflight errors through `plan_studio_builder_dispatch_execution_catalog` without launching builders, Studio host exposes that non-mutating readiness through `--builder-dispatch-execution-catalog`, editor action dispatch execution catalog planning reports explicit execution-admission readiness and per-action preflight errors through `plan_studio_editor_action_dispatch_execution_catalog` without launching editors, Studio host exposes that non-mutating readiness through `--editor-action-dispatch-execution-catalog`, toolbox dispatch execution catalog planning reports explicit execution-admission readiness and per-item preflight errors through `plan_studio_toolbox_dispatch_execution_catalog` without launching toolbox processes, and Studio host exposes that non-mutating readiness through `--toolbox-dispatch-execution-catalog`; selection-context toolbox palette invocation admission catalog planning now resolves Studio selections to toolbox palettes through `plan_studio_toolbox_invocation_admission_catalog_for_selection`, `plan_studio_toolbox_dispatch_catalog_for_selection` composes that admission catalog with non-executing dispatch planning, `plan_visual_object_from_toolbox_selection` resolves selected contexts into non-mutating single-item object create plans, `create_visual_object_from_toolbox_selection` and `create_visual_objects_from_toolbox_selection` execute selected-context single and ordered batch create plans through the existing VFP object append paths, `plan_visual_object_create_dispatch_from_toolbox_selection` composes selected-context single-item create plans with non-executing dispatch planning, `plan_visual_objects_from_toolbox_selection` resolves selected contexts into non-mutating ordered batch object create plans, `plan_visual_object_batch_create_dispatch_from_toolbox_selection` composes selected-context ordered batch create plans with non-executing dispatch planning, `plan_visual_object_catalog_from_toolbox_selection` resolves selected contexts into non-mutating object create-plan catalogs, and Studio host exposes `--selection-toolbox-invocation-admission-catalog`, `--selection-toolbox-dispatch-catalog`, `--selection-toolbox-create`, `--selection-toolbox-create-batch`, and `--selection-toolbox-create-batch-dispatch-plan` selected-context JSON surfaces with deterministic unsupported-context rejection before any UI execution, dispatch execution, or unintended asset mutation
- E2 designer/tooling execution-failure note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON preserves child reporting metadata on process-failure paths by using the pre-execution dispatch plan as a reporting-only fallback for child identity, command token, dispatch arguments, and executed command strings while keeping `execute_studio_designer_dispatch` free of stale aggregate success metadata.
- E2 designer/tooling failure-coverage note — aggregate designer execution JSON coverage now explicitly locks the failed editor-action, builder/wizard, and toolbox process-launch paths so all three child categories preserve planned child metadata after non-zero child exits.
- E2 designer/tooling execution-count note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now reports explicit editor-action, builder/wizard, and toolbox child execution entry counts beside aggregate execution/error totals so host consumers do not need to infer category totals from arrays or optional child objects.
- E2 designer/tooling error-count note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now reports explicit editor-action, builder/wizard, and toolbox child error counts beside aggregate error totals so host consumers can distinguish child failure distribution without scanning child result arrays.
- E2 designer/tooling success-count note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now reports explicit editor-action, builder/wizard, and toolbox child successful-execution counts beside aggregate execution totals so host consumers can distinguish partial-success distribution without scanning child result arrays.
- E2 designer/tooling failed-child-summary note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now reports compact failed editor-action ids, failed builder ids, and toolbox failure state beside detailed child execution entries so host consumers can identify failed child categories without scanning every child result.
- E2 designer/tooling failed-child-error-summary note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now reports compact failed editor-action errors, failed builder errors, and toolbox error text beside failed-child summaries so host consumers can diagnose failed child categories without scanning every child result.
- E2 designer/tooling child-exit-code note — Studio host execution JSON now normalizes `std::system(...)` wait statuses before reporting child `observedExitCode` values, so failed builder, editor-action, toolbox, and aggregate designer child launches report portable process exit codes instead of raw platform wait-status encodings.
- E2 designer/tooling child-launch-command note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now emits per-child `launchCommand` values for editor-action, builder/wizard, and toolbox entries beside dispatch arguments, executed commands, and observed exit-code metadata so aggregate child rows are self-contained for external hosts.
- E2 designer/tooling child-target-metadata note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now emits child-level target metadata for editor actions, builders/wizards, and toolbox execution entries, including selected asset/object identity and editor source-position details where applicable.
- E2 designer/tooling aggregate-toolbox-items note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now emits toolbox child `items` descriptors beside toolbox target metadata and execution telemetry, so external hosts can render toolbox child results without joining back to separate toolbox catalog output.
- E2 designer/tooling child-descriptor-metadata note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now emits editor-action label/kind and builder title/kind metadata on child rows, keeping aggregate execution child entries aligned with descriptor metadata needed by external designer hosts.
- E2 designer/tooling child-descriptor-detail note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now emits editor-action descriptions and builder VFP9-equivalent/component/description metadata on child rows, reducing the need for external hosts to join aggregate execution output back to descriptor catalogs.
- E2 designer/tooling failed-child-command-token note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now emits compact failed child command-token summaries for editor-action, builder/wizard, and toolbox failures beside failed ids/errors and detailed child rows.
- E2 designer/tooling failed-child-executed-command note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now emits compact failed child executed-command summaries for editor-action, builder/wizard, and toolbox failures beside failed ids/tokens/errors and detailed child rows.
- E2 designer/tooling failed-child-exit-code note — Studio host aggregate designer execution JSON now emits compact numeric failed child exit-code summaries for editor-action, builder/wizard, and toolbox failures beside failed ids/tokens/commands/errors and detailed child rows.
- E2 designer/tooling dispatch-execution-catalog-readiness-summary note — Studio host designer dispatch execution catalog JSON now emits compact ready and blocked selection-context summaries with blocked execution errors beside detailed per-context readiness entries.
- E2 designer/tooling dispatch-catalog-status-summary note — Studio host designer dispatch catalog JSON now emits compact dispatch-clean and dispatch-blocked selection-context summaries with representative blocked dispatch errors beside detailed per-context dispatch entries.
- E2 designer/tooling invocation-admission-catalog-status-summary note — Studio host designer invocation admission catalog JSON now emits compact admission-clean and admission-blocked selection-context summaries with representative blocked admission errors beside detailed per-context invocation admission entries.

- Runtime-host packaging/security notes — security-enabled package startup now preserves recorded extension-payload relative-path fidelity during digest verification, so same-named files at the package root cannot satisfy nested payload hashes outside the recorded package location
